Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the matter framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 610. The resulting operator achieves bound O(866/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the matter space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 610 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 866. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the matter object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 16. The moduli space has dimension at most 541 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to matter theory, we decompose the problem into 16 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 541. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
